CURRENT AUTHORITY OVERRIDE — POST-229AJ SMOOTH-INERT FOUR-GAP PACKETIZATION
LATEST_POST_229_DELTA_OVERRIDE: Stage37-NEW-INPUT-DELTA-AUDIT-229AJ. Operational authority remains M3-T11-229; latest mathematical construction remains M3-T11-228. T11-230 remains unissued.


NEW EXACT ADAPTERS:
  G_* = gcd(eR,xy) = gcd(UV,xy) = a b c12 c21,
  K_* = sf(gcd(UV,xy)).
For the odd inert part, define the unique realized packets a_-,a_+,b_-,b_+ by assigning each p^k||a, p==3 mod4 to the unique gap R-x or R+x having valuation2k, and each p^k||b to the unique gap R-y or R+y having valuation2k. With A_±=rad(a_±), B_±=rad(b_±),
  rad_3(K_*) = A_- A_+ B_- B_+,
and the four factors are pairwise coprime.


SMOOTH-INERT RECEIVER:
For every T>=1,
  rad_3(K_*)>T => max(A_-,A_+,B_-,B_+)>T^(1/4).
Thus at T=B^(1/3-delta), a sufficient inert-side receiver is that the sum of the four same-physical packet counts above B^(1/12-delta/4) is o(B^(1/3)). This covers the smooth inert radical case left open by229AI; the required packet count itself is NOT proved.


FIREWALL:
On the primitive quotients of (U,y,R) and (V,x,R), Euclid parameterization gives each of R±y and R±x as c12 or c21 times a square, or twice that content times a square. Therefore generic large-square-divisor sparsity on these linear gaps is not an independent saving. Any quantitative use must preserve the realized a/b packet identity, nested Pythagorean coupling, and exact R-height.


CURRENT_PREFERRED_GATE=UNCHANGED:
  #{positive primitive physical endpoints R<=B :
      sf(gcd(eR,xy)) > B^(1/3-delta)}
    = o(B^(1/3))
for some fixed delta>0.

CURRENT AUTHORITY OVERRIDE — POST-229AK CROSSED FACE-SPACE EUCLID-ROOT INTERSECTION


LATEST_POST_229_DELTA_OVERRIDE: Stage37-NEW-INPUT-DELTA-AUDIT-229AK.
LATEST_POST_229_DELTA_DOC_ID=1R0wp-dLJ4ZnIXebQ-6QLhreNs-za2O-a_ddP7rM-gDM.
PREVIOUS_POST_229_DELTA=Stage37-NEW-INPUT-DELTA-AUDIT-229AJ.
OPERATIONAL_AUTHORITY=M3-T11-229.
LATEST_MATHEMATICAL_CONSTRUCTION=M3-T11-228.


229AK exact joint refinement:
- For primitive face T_a=(ga,d0 c,n1), its Euclid-root product recovers a on odd gcd with y: gcd_odd(r_{a,-}r_{a,+},y)=a_odd. Similarly T_b=(gb,d0 f,n2) gives gcd_odd(r_{b,-}r_{b,+},x)=b_odd.
- Intersecting these face-root signs with the clean229AJ space-root signs from (V,x,R)/c21 and (U,y,R)/c12 partitions the complete odd inert a/b support into eight pairwise-coprime packets a_{sigma,tau}, b_{sigma,tau}.
- Exact product:
    rad_3(K_*)=product_{sigma,tau} rad(a_{sigma,tau}) rad(b_{sigma,tau}).
- Every a-packet m satisfies simultaneously
    m^2 | n1+sigma d0 c,
    m^2 | R+tau x;
  every b-packet satisfies
    m^2 | n2+sigma d0 f,
    m^2 | R+tau y.
Thus each inert packet is a same-physical common Euclid-root packet across one primitive face triangle and the crossed space triangle.


QUANTITATIVE STATUS:
- rad_3(K_*)>T implies one of the eight packet radicals>T^(1/8); at T=B^(1/3-delta), threshold=B^(1/24-delta/8).
- This does NOT supersede229AJ. The 229AJ four-packet receiver at B^(1/12-delta/4) remains the preferred inert structural receiver.
- 229AK is useful only if a same-physical theorem extracts a genuine extra saving from the simultaneous face/space common-root incidence. The two square-divisibility conditions arise from the same a/b content and must not be multiplied as independent local densities.
- No counting promotion; no production reopen.


CURRENT_PREFERRED_GATE remains:
  #{positive primitive physical endpoints R<=B :
      sf(gcd(eR,xy)) > B^(1/3-delta)}
    = o(B^(1/3))
for some fixed delta>0.

229AR macro-Pythagorean whole-support refinement:

    (eR)^2+(xy)^2=(UV)^2.

    G_*=gcd(eR,xy)=gcd(eR,UV)=gcd(xy,UV).
  Thus
    K_*=sf(G_*)=sf(gcd(eR,UV)).
- After primitive normalization there exist epsilon_macro in {0,1} and coprime r_-<r_+ with
    UV-eR=2^epsilon_macro G_* r_-^2,
    UV+eR=2^epsilon_macro G_* r_+^2,
    gcd(UV-eR,UV+eR)=2^epsilon_macro G_*.
- Therefore
    K_odd=sf_odd(UV-eR)=sf_odd(UV+eR).
  So K_*>T implies both single-scalar odd kernels exceed T/2. At T=B^(1/3-delta), this preserves the full B^(1/3-delta) threshold with no packet-threshold loss.
- Height tradeoff:
    0<UV-eR<B^2,
    0<UV+eR<2B^2.
  This no-threshold-loss quadratic-height receiver is complementary to229AQ's two O(B)-height linear packets at B^(1/6-delta/2).

    L_-L_+=2(UV+eR)=2^(epsilon_macro+1)G_*r_+^2,
    lambda(e+U+V+R)=2(UV-eR)=2^(epsilon_macro+1)G_*r_-^2.
  Thus both229AQ products carry raw G_* times a square and one controlled power of2.

229BM exact symmetric completion:

  O(lambda_j)=Q_j^- square,
  O(Sigma_j)=Q_j^+ square,

and

229BN WHOLE-POPULATION DUAL COFACTOR TRIANGLE:
  H0:=xy/G0=d0^2XY,
  H1:=ex/G1=b^2EX,

Exactly,
  gcd(H0,H1)=X,
  gcd(H0,H2)=Y,
  gcd(H1,H2)=E,
  gcd(H0,H1,H2)=1,
  H0H1H2=(abd0EXY)^2.
Therefore the H-triple recovers X,Y,E and then d0,b,a uniquely by square roots.


FOUR-COORDINATE SUPPORT:
  Xi(P)=(G0,H0,H1,H2).
For fixed realized Xi,
  Cprod=G0/(ab)=c12c21.
There are at most tau(Cprod) ordered divisor splits. Each split fixes c21,c12,c,f,x,y and at most one q through (bc)^2+(af)^2=q^2. Then h|q gives at most tau(q) choices; g,e,z,R,U,V are forced/check-only. Since Cprod<=B^2 and q<=B,
  #Xi^{-1}(xi)=B^o(1)
uniformly.


LARGE-RADICAL PUSHFORWARD:
For Xi_B^large(delta) with sf(G0)>B^(1/3-delta),
  #P_large(B;delta)<=B^o(1)#Xi_B^large(delta).
Thus #Xi_B^large<<B^(1/3-epsilon) with fixed epsilon>0 is sufficient.

229BO TWO-COORDINATE RECONSTRUCTION:
  Kx=EX=H1/b^2,
  Ky=EY=H2/a^2.
For fixed (G0,Kx), factor G0=a b c12 c21 and Kx=EX. Then A=b c21 X and C=a c12 are fixed and
  (q-CY)(q+CY)=A^2
gives only B^o(1) choices for Y,q. Next h|q, e=hEab, and
  (R-d0q)(R+d0q)=e^2
gives only B^o(1) choices for d0,R. Remaining coordinates are forced/check-only. Thus fixed (G0,Kx) fiber=B^o(1); symmetric fixed (G0,Ky) theorem also holds.


229BO ONE-SCALAR RECEIVER:
  Theta_x=G0*Kx^2,
  Theta_y=G0*Ky^2.
Exactly
  sf(Theta_x)=sf(Theta_y)=sf(G0),
  Theta_x,Theta_y<=B^6.
For fixed theta, there are at most tau(theta)=B^o(1) admissible G0 divisors with theta/G0 square; K is then fixed, and the two-coordinate theorem gives B^o(1) physical preimages. Therefore fixed-Theta fiber=B^o(1).


LARGE-RADICAL PUSHFORWARD:
  #P_large(B;delta)<=B^o(1)#T_x(B;delta),
where T_x is the realized Theta_x value set and sf(theta)>B^(1/3-delta) exactly. A fixed-power bound #T_x<<B^(1/3-epsilon) is sufficient.


COMPARISON:
229AR W_- is lower height O(B^2) and squareclass-preserving but fixed-W_- fiber remains unresolved.
229BO Theta is coarser height O(B^6) but source multiplicity is solved.
